## Cinematic Woman and Cat Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A cinematic, magazine-cover quality portrait of a {argument name="ethnicity" default="young South Asian woman"} with long, flowing dark hair, wearing an {argument name="outfit" default="elegant black long-sleeved top"}. She is gently holding a {argument name="pet" default="fluffy, long-haired white Persian cat"}, looking at it with a soft, affectionate expression. Dramatic rim lighting from behind beautifully illuminates the edges of her hair and the cat's soft fur, creating a glowing halo effect. Subtle mist or fine dust particles are caught in the sharp backlight against a stark, pitch-black background. Shot on an 85mm lens at f/1.8, shallow depth of field, intense cinematic bokeh, hyper-realistic textures, 8K resolution, editorial fashion style. --ar 4:5
```

## Luxury Indoor Menswear Lookbook

> Lüks moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A confident male in his thirties stands deliberately posed with his body turned at a forty-five-degree angle, centering his weight with level shoulders. His hair features a high taper fade on the sides with a three-to-four-inch swept-back pompadour on top, styled with a matte product that reveals natural texture and subtle flyaways. He wears a {argument name="clothing" default="dark charcoal gray textured suit"} over an unbuttoned crisp white shirt. His expression is sophisticated and composed, with a neutral mouth, no smile, and relaxed eyebrows, while his eyes are concealed behind {argument name="accessory" default="round tortoiseshell sunglasses"} directed toward the left. His left hand, adorned with a silver watch and a gold ring, has fingers slightly curled as it grasps the left lapel of his jacket. His right hand, styled with silver and black bracelets and a silver ring, rests lightly on the right edge of the jacket near the button, creating an asymmetric visual hierarchy that draws the eye down the open collar to his hands and jewelry. He stands within a pristine indoor luxury interior of medium spatial depth, resting on a floor of {argument name="setting" default="solid white marble tile"} accented by gray veining. In the left midground, a prominent staircase built of matching white marble with gray veins features a glass railing and elegant brass trim. Behind him to the right, dominant warm cream sheer drapes hang in pristine condition, their soft vertical pleats creating vertical light and shadow streaks across the matte fabric. A single source of soft, natural window light streams in from ninety degrees to the camera left, establishing a sophisticated and luxurious mood with a warm temperature. This moderately directional light casts soft, gradual, short gray shadows across the right side of his face and body, while pristine preserved highlights illuminate his left cheek, the crisp white shirt collar, and his metallic jewelry. The ambient fill balances the medium contrast, accentuating a neutral but warm color profile heavily dominated by the charcoal gray of his suit, the crisp white of the shirt and stairs, warm cream drapes, and natural skin tones. Captured in the style of a high-end editorial menswear lookbook and luxury lifestyle photography using an 85mm lens set to f/2.8 at 1/200s and ISO 100, the realistic digital image boasts tack-sharp focus on the subject with a slightly soft background depth of field, finished with a subtle warm color grade, refined contrast enhancement, and localized sharpening on his face and jewelry to ensure a clean aesthetic, all perfectly framed in a 4:5 aspect ratio.
```

## Urban Rooftop Sunset Portrait

> Lüks mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
The person from the reference photo is seated on a metal rooftop railing overlooking a cityscape at {argument name="time of day" default="sunset"}, with the torso angled slightly to the right and the spine upright but relaxed. Both hands are gently holding a mug near chest height. The legs are bent at the knees, with the left foot resting on a lower metal bar and the right foot placed slightly forward on the same bar, creating a casual, balanced seated posture. The head is turned slightly to the left with the gaze directed toward the horizon, wearing a calm, contemplative expression. dark sunglasses with thin metallic frames complete the look.

They are wearing a {argument name="jacket" default="charcoal-gray casual blazer"} layered over a {argument name="top" default="black crew-neck top"}, paired with slim black trousers. Black low-top sneakers with white socks complete the outfit, matching the urban rooftop setting.

The environment is a hazy, warm-toned city skyline at sunset, with tall buildings fading into atmospheric haze in the background. A long avenue below is filled with lines of bokeh traffic lights, stretching toward the horizon. The sky is a muted golden color, casting soft, diffused light across the scene. The metal railing structure is weathered, adding an industrial edge to the atmosphere.

Full-body portrait in portrait orientation, captured from an eye-level camera angle with a slight three-quarter perspective. The person is centered, with the city depth unfolding dramatically behind them. Depth of field keeps the subject crisp while allowing the city lights and skyline to blur smoothly into atmospheric distance.

Professional editorial photography, photorealistic. Shot on a Canon EOS 5D Mark IV with an 85mm f/1.8 lens; aperture around f/2.0 for pronounced background bokeh and strong subject isolation. Natural optical depth of field with realistic blur; crisp subject focus. Physically plausible warm sunset lighting with soft shadows and gentle tonal transitions. True-to-life colors with warm, hazy grading consistent with an urban sunset. Subtle photographer-style post-processing, realistic color grading, moderate contrast. High-resolution, ultra-detailed, sharp image quality (8k). Natural matte skin texture, visible pores, subtle micro-details; no plastic smoothing.

NEGATIVE PROMPT: CGI, 3D render, fake, artificial, synthetic, plastic skin, wax skin, rubbery skin, airbrushed skin, over-smoothing, AI artifacts, digital artifacts, jpeg artifacts, pixelation, distorted anatomy, extra limbs, deformed, disfigured, mutated, heavy makeup, beauty filters, glamour retouching, overly youthful/teenage appearance, unnatural glow, fake shine, extreme cinematic lighting, harsh HDR, motion blur, out of focus (unless intentionally present), distortion, out of frame.
```

## Ethereal Motion Blur Portrait

> Sinematik, retro portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A soft-focus, cinematic portrait of a young woman with delicate facial features and light golden hair, caught in motion. Her face appears partially blurred and ghosted, with flowing strands of hair captured mid-sway, creating a dreamy, ethereal motion effect. Her expression is serene and slightly distant, with parted lips and eyes softly open.The entire frame is bathed in golden beige and sepia tones, evoking a nostalgic, painterly atmosphere. The lighting is natural and diffused, coming from a nearby window, casting a warm glow on her skin and illuminating the strands of hair that dance across the frame. Photographed with a Canon EOS R5 using an 85mm lens at f/1.2, ISO 320, shutter speed 1/8s with intentional slow exposure and handheld movement to create the artistic motion blur. The focus is soft, with a shallow depth of field that isolates the subject from the pale, creamy background. Post-processed in Adobe Lightroom and Photoshop: cinematic LUTs applied, warmth enhanced through split-toning (shadows in soft amber, highlights in cream), clarity reduced, and motion streaks refined with displacement layers and radial blur filters. A very subtle fine grain adds a filmic mood: emotional, hazy, poetic. Style references: fine art portraiture, vintage editorial, and analog dream sequences. 4K ultra-resolution, no harsh contrasts, smooth gradients preserved.
```

A wrecked {argument name="car color" default="COLOR"} {argument name="car model" default="CAR MODEL"} lies partially sunk into deep snow, the front end swallowed by ice, metal twisted and buried. Mounted on the rear bumper, a bent and slightly frosted license plate is clearly visible, reading "{argument name="license plate" default="LICENSE PLATE"}". Snow particles float in the air, caught mid-fall, the atmosphere is cold, silent, hostile. Hyperrealistic detail, gritty cinematic and dramatic contrast.
```

```
A candid, serious profile shot of a {argument name="subject" default="male subject"} leaning casually into the left side of the frame, his relaxed posture supported by shoulders angled naturally. His hair is meticulously styled with three to four inches on top blending into a sharp tapered fade on the sides and back, held in place by a medium-hold pomade that catches subtle highlights along natural comb lines, while a few slight, organic flyaways escape at the crown. He is dressed in a {argument name="clothing" default="black long-sleeve knit shirt and cream trousers"}, his face partially shielded by translucent-frame sunglasses as he gazes away with a neutral, relaxed expression. His left hand rests organically on the top edge of a glass railing with the wrist slightly bent, while his right hand rests comfortably beside it with gently curled fingers adorned by a gold link bracelet and two gold rings. He leans against a pristine, warm gray matte concrete walkway ledge dotted with thriving, manicured green shrubs, observing the deep spatial expanse of the scene before him. In the pristine teal-blue midground waterway, a moving speedboat carves a white wake beneath a red and white flag, passing an anchored, weathered traditional brown wooden dhow with white trim on the right. The background rises majestically into a dominant {argument name="building" default="red terracotta high-rise building"} characterized by distinctive tiered architecture and geometric windows, flanked by various sand-colored resort structures. The serene and contemplative urban evening is bathed in soft, diffused, single-source light cascading neutrally from above, casting short, soft-edged gray shadows gently beneath his jawline and arms while catching delicate highlights on the sheen of his hair product, sunglass frames, and the rippling water below. The cool, moderately saturated split-complementary color palette beautifully contrasts the deep black of his knitwear against the sweeping teal water, warm terracotta skyline, and warm beige accents. Captured straight-on via crisp digital photography using a 50mm lens at f/4.0, ISO 400, and a 1/250s shutter speed to achieve a medium depth of field and tack-sharp focus with a whisper of realistic digital noise, the image is refined by a modern luxury lifestyle aesthetic featuring subtle teal and orange color grading, gently lifted shadows, and clean natural skin retouching, all framed in a 9:16 aspect ratio.
```

## Mediterranean Historic Site Male Portrait

> Mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A handsome male in his {argument name="age" default="mid-30s"} with an athletic build stands in a confident, deliberately posed stance, his direct gaze meeting the lens with a sophisticated, neutral expression. His {argument name="hair description" default="medium-length, wavy brown hair"} is pushed back, revealing natural texture and subtle strands caught in the sun. He is dressed in casual elegance, wearing {argument name="outfit" default="a fitted, dark charcoal grey button-down shirt with rolled-up sleeves, sharply tailored bright white trousers, and tan suede slip-on loafers"}. His body is gently angled, weight resting comfortably to the left as his right shoulder leans against a massive stone structure, with one leg casually crossed over the other at the ankle. Both hands are relaxed in front of his waist, loosely clasping the left and right sides of a pair of dark sunglasses, with his fingers lightly gripping the frame. He is anchored in an ancient Mediterranean historical site, specifically leaning against a dominant, highly weathered square pillar in the left foreground constructed of large, irregular, porous grey stone blocks complete with deeply pitted surfaces and visible rough masonry joints. The mid-level spatial depth reveals an earthy tan and brown floor of irregular cobblestones and flat stone slabs. To the right midground sits a worn, rectangular cut-stone block bench with subtle panel details, and just behind it, a classic aged stone archway frames a brightly sunlit, heavily textured interior wall crafted from raw stone and plaster. The scene is bathed in hard, highly directional natural sunlight cascading from the front-left and slightly elevated, creating a warm, energetic, and sun-drenched atmosphere. This intense, golden single-source light sculpts high-contrast, harsh-edged, deep black shadows, deliberately casting the man's silhouette directly onto the rough stone pillar behind his right side and across the cobblestone ground. Bright, preserved highlights strike his left cheek, his left shoulder, the crisp folds of his white trousers, and the vivid ochre and yellow-orange textured earth of the distant arched wall, establishing a split-complementary palette that beautifully contrasts the cool stone greys with warm, vibrant accents. Captured as a photorealistic, tack-sharp digital editorial travel lifestyle portrait using a 50mm lens with a deep depth of field, ensuring both the subject and the architectural background remain perfectly in focus. The aesthetic features a high contrast curve to emphasize the dramatic interplay of hard light and deep shadow, along with a saturation boost on the warm golden and ochre tones to create distinct color separation and emphasize the raw, porous textures of the masonry and clothing fabrics, all perfectly framed in a 4:5 aspect ratio.
```

His posture is deliberately posed, shoulders tilted as his body turns at a 45-degree angle with his weight shifted, leaning casually. His left hand is tucked into the front left pocket of his dark denim, with the thumb hooked inside the pocket edge, knuckles visible, and fingers resting relaxed on the outside fabric, while his right arm extends upward, the hand resting flat against the fluted mid-section of an ornate cast iron lamppost, fingers gently curling to wrap around the metal molding in a relaxed grip. This heavy, dark charcoal-painted foreground lamppost on the left, showing signs of weathered dust and wear along its decorative scrollwork base, visually balances his leaning posture and guides the eye through a deep, bustling {argument name="location" default="Parisian street intersection"}. A worn white commercial panel van and a silver car wait in the midground traffic over medium warm gray concrete marked with white lines, while distant pedestrians navigate beneath bare, weathered branches. Towering in the background are vintage Haussmann-style buildings featuring warm sandstone-beige, slightly textured matte plaster facades aged with architectural moldings, tall arched windows, wrought iron Juliet balconies, shop awnings. An energetic atmosphere is established by bright, highly directional natural sunlight striking from a front 45-degree angle, casting a warm golden glow over the complementary color palette. This single hard light source creates specular highlights on the rounded metal details of the lamppost, the left shoulder of the leather jacket, and the subject's forehead and hair, while carving out deep black, harshly defined shadows under his chin, on the right side of his face and nose, across his left leg cast by his right leg, and stretching directly onto the street surface to the right of the subject and lamppost. Captured with digital photography mimicking a 35mm to 50mm lens at an aperture between f/4.0 and f/5.6 for a medium depth of field, this low-angle editorial street style portrait utilizes a fast 1/1000s shutter speed at ISO 100. The image is meticulously post-processed with a warm color temperature adjustment and a high contrast curve, increasing clarity and texture to highlight the tack-sharp, grain-free material details of the clothing and cast iron, structured around the rule of thirds for an asymmetric balance, and naturally framed in a 4:5 aspect ratio.
```

## Summer Beach Editorial Portrait

> Portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A {argument name="subject" default="handsome young adult male"} exudes carefree summer energy sitting slightly right of center and leaning comfortably back onto warm gray lounge cushions. He is dressed in a {argument name="outfit" default="highly textured warm cream knit polo with pushed-up sleeves and crisp white linen pants"}, accessorized with a delicate pearl necklace. His tan skin glows, and his dark hair is styled in a natural, windswept textured quiff with subtle flyaways catching the light. His head is turned in profile to the left, his eyes relaxed and squinting slightly as he gazes thoughtfully out of frame, his face lit up by a broad, candid, open-mouth smile showing his upper teeth. His relaxed left hand rests naturally over his right hand in his lap, fingers gently draped over the bottom hand's loosely curled inward fingers, prominently displaying a silver watch with a bright, clear blue dial and matching strap. Beneath him in the foreground, pristine, smooth beige cushions fold naturally, catching deep cast shadows from his weight. Beyond the crisp foreground, the midground transitions into golden, uneven textured sand dotted with subtle ridges and footprints, curving toward a small, deep blue sliver of ocean visible at the center-left edge. The deep spatial depth concludes with a gently blurred background of towering rocky coastal hills and dunes covered in sparse dry vegetation, sloping downward toward the sweeping clear blue sky that fills the left side of the frame to create an asymmetric balance. Highly directional, hard natural golden sunlight blazes from a 45-degree angle on camera-left, creating a highly saturated, complementary color palette of warm creams, golden tans, and vibrant blues. The intense, single-source light preserves brilliant highlights on the left side of his face, left shoulder, nose, and a sharp specular gleam on the watch face, while casting dramatic, dense, deep black shadows with harsh, defined edges across the right side of his face, beneath his chin, and down the right side of his torso. Captured with realistic digital photography using a portrait lens at a wide aperture, the tack-sharp focus on the subject creates a medium depth of field that beautifully separates him from the softly blurred coastal backdrop. The straight-on perspective, completely free of film grain, mimics a clean, contemporary golden hour vacation editorial with enhanced warmth and high-contrast color grading to make the cool blues pop against the golden skin tones, all flawlessly framed in a 4:5 aspect ratio.
```

He deliberately leans his weight back onto his right leg against a vehicle, his shoulders level and relaxed. His left hand is tucked casually into his front left pants pocket with the wrist and part of the thumb visibly resting on his hip line, while his right hand rests naturally on the edge of the car trunk, his fingers gently curling downward over the glossy panel edge. Behind him in the left midground sits a sleek, dark grey convertible sports car with the top down, all parked on dark gray asphalt with a rough aggregate texture. The deep spatial background unveils a historic European city street framed by warm beige and light sandstone gray carved stone buildings showcasing a matte, weathered finish, large classical archways, decorative pillars, ornate moldings, and attached street lamps. In the slightly softened background, pedestrians walk by, including one wearing a bright pinkish-red coat that subtly echoes the glowing car. The sophisticated, opulent urban evening is bathed in soft, natural outdoor ambient light diffused from above, supplemented by the practical glow of the taillights and a heavy ambient bounce from the historic masonry. This creates a medium contrast ratio where the lighting remains flat on the subject but produces a stark tonal separation between the deep blacks of the primary subjects and the warm stone background. Short shadows with soft, gradual edges pool into deep blacks under the car and within the suit folds, while crisp specular highlights gleam across the vehicle's curves and slightly catch the man's hair. Captured as a clean, structured commercial lifestyle photograph using an 85mm lens from a straight-on, slightly low angle, the shot utilizes an aperture between f/2.8 and f/4 at ISO 100 to ensure tack-sharp focus on the foreground elements while maintaining a medium depth of field. The cinematic post-processing crushes the blacks to enrich the suit and car paint, subtly desaturates the background's warm tones to isolate the subject, boosts the luminance of the red taillights, and applies a sharp high-pass filter to the man, all elegantly framed in a {argument name="aspect ratio" default="1:1"} aspect ratio.

He is positioned on a sandy gray-brown gravel driveway in an outdoor desert setting with deep spatial depth, structured with asymmetric balance following the rule of thirds. Dominating the immediate right foreground is the pristine side profile of a {argument name="vehicle" default="white Jeep Wrangler"} detailed with stark black door handles, side mirrors, and fender flares. Framing the left midground is a small, thriving brownish-gray spiky desert shrub, leading back to a luxurious, minimalist flat-roofed modern house. The house boasts perfectly smooth, matte off-white plaster walls, accented by warm wood paneling around the entryway and a dark garage door segmented by horizontal glass panels. The scene is bathed in a calm, soft, diffused front light from a single neutral dusk source, creating a quiet, modern desert lifestyle atmosphere. Soft, gradual gray shadows pool shortly beneath the subject and cast lightly onto the gravel behind him, while perfectly preserved specular highlights touch his forehead, the bridge of his nose, and dance subtly as reflections along the car body. The color palette is high-contrast yet beautifully balances the deep black of the subject's clothing against the off-white architecture, the pale blue twilight sky above, and the warm sand hues of the earth below. Captured as a tack-sharp digital photograph on an 85mm lens at f/4.0 and ISO 400, the medium depth of field keeps the subject and vehicle sharply focused while subtly softening the distant house. Drawing influence from contemporary luxury automotive and apparel editorial photography, the clean image features post-processing with lifted shadows to retain intricate details in the dark clothing and refined color grading that accentuates the cool twilight blues and warm grounded sand tones, all seamlessly framed in a 4:5 aspect ratio.

## Luxury Car Lifestyle Photography

> Fütüristik, lüks mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A {argument name="subject" default="young, well-groomed man"} in a fitted matte black suit, crisp white shirt, and floral tie stands positioned in the left vertical third of the frame, cut just at the top of his shoes. His confident, guarded expression is shielded by dark round sunglasses that catch a distinct specular highlight, his head turned forty-five degrees to his left while his gaze is directed away to the right. He deliberately leans his weight back onto his right leg against a vehicle, his shoulders level and relaxed. His left hand is tucked casually into his front left pants pocket with the wrist and part of the thumb visibly resting on his hip line, while his right hand rests naturally on the edge of the car trunk, his fingers gently curling downward over the glossy panel edge. He is leaning against a {argument name="vehicle" default="pristine, glossy black Porsche Panamera Turbo S"} that dominates the center and right midground, featuring a deployed spoiler and a prominently illuminated neon red LED taillight bar highlighting sharp chrome typography with a wide-spaced PORSCHE and a flowing script Panamera turbo S. Behind him in the left midground sits a sleek, dark grey convertible sports car with the top down, all parked on dark gray asphalt with a rough aggregate texture. The {argument name="background" default="deep spatial background unveils a historic European city street"} framed by warm beige and light sandstone gray carved stone buildings showcasing a matte, weathered finish, large classical archways, decorative pillars, ornate moldings, and attached street lamps. In the slightly softened background, pedestrians walk by, including one wearing a bright pinkish-red coat that subtly echoes the glowing car. The sophisticated, opulent urban evening is bathed in soft, natural outdoor ambient light diffused from above, supplemented by the practical glow of the taillights and a heavy ambient bounce from the historic masonry. This creates a medium contrast ratio where the lighting remains flat on the subject but produces a stark tonal separation between the deep blacks of the primary subjects and the warm stone background. Short shadows with soft, gradual edges pool into deep blacks under the car and within the suit folds, while crisp specular highlights gleam across the vehicle's curves and slightly catch the man's hair. Captured as a clean, structured commercial lifestyle photograph using an 85mm lens from a straight-on, slightly low angle, the shot utilizes an aperture between f/2.8 and f/4 at ISO 100 to ensure tack-sharp focus on the foreground elements while maintaining a medium depth of field. The cinematic post-processing crushes the blacks to enrich the suit and car paint, subtly desaturates the background's warm tones to isolate the subject, boosts the luminance of the red taillights, and applies a sharp high-pass filter to the man, all elegantly framed in a 1:1 aspect ratio.
```

Captured straight on as a tack-sharp, grain-free realistic digital photograph using an 85mm lens at an aperture between f/2.8 and f/4 at ISO 100, the image employs a medium depth of field that keeps the primary subject impeccably crisp while rendering the background environment softly blurred yet identifiable. A muscular {argument name="subject" default="male"} with short textured hair and a groomed beard stands fully in frame, exuding a confident, posed demeanor with a direct gaze, neutral eyebrows, and a closed mouth. He is sharply dressed in a {argument name="outfit" default="fitted navy blue button-down shirt, crisp white trousers secured by a brown leather belt, and brown woven leather loafers"}, accessorized with a men's wristwatch and beaded bracelets. Facing the camera straight on with his weight casually shifted and shoulders level, he leans against the focal point of his environment. His relaxed left hand rests naturally on a car's window sill, his fingers slightly curled inwards, while his right hand gently holds a pair of dark aviator sunglasses by the frames near his belt buckle. He interacts directly with a pristine, {argument name="car" default="sage green metallic Aston Martin Vantage sports car"} dominating the midground, featuring a large front grille and bronze-colored alloy wheels. The deep, layered spatial environment rests on a solid slate grey tiled floor. To the left, a pristine clear glass balustrade with minimal metal structural posts overlooks the scenery, while to the right stands a weathered, light tan and grey rough stone wall, distinguished by roughly hewn rectangular blocks, irregular joints, natural stone pitting, and a patchy matte finish. Thriving green pine tree needles partially enter the top right foreground, framing a deep background of a mountainous landscape with rolling green hills and scattered white buildings. The scene captures a high-end, luxurious, yet polished European coastal lifestyle mood, saturated in a split-complementary color palette where sage green, navy blue, crisp white, and slate grey heavily dominate. Bright, single-source natural daylight diffuses softly from above, casting medium contrast and a neutral light temperature across the scene. Short, soft-edged gray shadows fall gently under the subject and the car on the slate tiles, while subtle specular highlights gleam off the Aston Martin's metallic paint and the man's forehead and nose, balanced by a gentle ambient fill. Captured as a tack-sharp, grain-free digital commercial photograph, the image mimics the sophisticated, clean visual style of GQ Magazine and luxury automotive lifestyle photography. It is shot on a 50mm lens at f/5.6 for a moderate depth of field, utilizing ISO 100 and a 1/250s shutter speed, complete with subtle post-processing color grading to enhance the sage and navy tones, crisp contrast applied to the clothing, and slightly lifted shadows for a flawlessly clean aesthetic, all perfectly framed in a 4:5 aspect ratio.

## Grit Late Night Parking Lot Fashion

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
McDonald’s parking: A gritty handheld iPhone flash photo shot late at night in a McDonald’s parking lot. A man is sitting on the rear edge of a parked orange Porsche 911 GT3 RS. He uses the car’s rear spoiler as an improvised table — fast-food bags, fries, cups, and crumpled wrappers spread across the spoiler. His posture is relaxed and unposed: one leg bent, the other hanging naturally, shoulders loose, body slightly slouched. He is wearing a sharp, tailored formal outfit — a structured dark blazer over a clean shirt, pressed trousers, polished shoes — clearly elegant and intentional. The contrast between formal menswear and junk food is deliberate, ironic, and effortless. Behind him, the McDonald’s building glows against the dark night sky. Lighting is raw and imperfect: harsh direct on-camera flash blowing out highlights on skin and fabric, hard shadows falling onto asphalt, uneven exposure, visible grain, slight motion blur. Feels accidental, blunt, and real — a late-night moment caught without preparation. Yakut Arctic: A chaotic, flash-lit winter scene set inside a real, lived-in igloo in the Yakut Arctic, blending dark humor with raw, rebellious energy. Use the attached portrait as the exact facial reference and preserve the face with 100% accuracy — no changes to facial structure, proportions, expression, skin texture, age, or identity. The face must remain perfectly identical, ultra-sharp, and fully photorealistic. The main character is a man, sitting slouched at a low improvised table inside the igloo, surrounded by thick fur blankets, carved ice walls, simple wooden objects, thermoses, and everyday household items adapted for extreme cold. The space feels genuinely inhabited, not stylized — practical, worn, and warm despite the frozen surroundings. Behind him, a white Porsche 911 GT3 RS has crashed through the ice wall of the igloo, shattering ice blocks and snow inward. Cracked ice, frozen debris, and powdery snow scatter across the floor. The car’s metallic body and glowing red taillights violently contrast with the white-blue ice interior and subzero environment. He wears an oversized patterned knit sweater, dark thermal pants, heavy winter socks, and a red Santa hat tilted casually on his head. One leg rests on the table, elbow on knee, chin leaning into his hand. His expression is bored, unimpressed, and defiant — as if nothing about the chaos surprises him anymore. Outside the broken igloo walls, the endless frozen Yakut landscape stretches into darkness, with extreme cold visible in the air. Harsh on-camera flash freezes every detail — ice texture, fabric fibers, frost, and dust — creating gritty realism with slight grain and imperfect exposure. Ironic holiday mood, cultural contrast, cinematic mess, underground fashion editorial energy. Face must remain unchanged and 100% preserved. Direct flash, grainy film look, dark festive chaos, rebellious holiday aesthetic, Yakut Arctic, residential igloo, Porsche 911 GT3.

## Retro Nightlife Flash Portrait

> Retro portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A young, athletic adult male styled in retro-modern evening resort wear stands with his hips pressed backward against a vehicle, his torso leaning asymmetrically toward the right while his shoulders slouch slightly back. His warm tan skin contrasts with a {argument name="shirt style" default="bright white, unbuttoned dress shirt"} that opens in a deep V-shape, paired with {argument name="pants style" default="cream off-white trousers"}. His slicked-back hair is sharply illuminated, and his head turns sharply 45 degrees to the left, his gaze directed off-camera and his eyes completely obscured by thick square black wayfarer-style sunglasses. His expression is aloof, confident, and serious, featuring a neutral, unsmiling mouth. His left hand rests naturally on his left thigh in the lower center frame, fingers loosely curled inward over his knee, revealing a silver wristwatch with a gold-tone dial. He leans heavily against the solid midground structure of a {argument name="car style" default="vintage mint green and white coupe car"}. The vehicle exhibits an aged, slightly oxidized matte-satin finish across its mint green body, detailing a vertical door seam, horizontal trim lines, and a silver chrome door handle, while its off-white roof reveals visible patchy dust spots and environmental wear. Below him, the solid, rough texture of the light gray paved asphalt floor catches the immediate illumination. Behind the vehicle, the background transitions into dense, broad-leafed trees and foliage that remain completely unlit, fading seamlessly into the deep black void of a retro coastal night sky. A single-source, highly directional on-camera flash pierces the darkness with harsh, daylight-balanced hard light, creating a highly energetic nightlife atmosphere. This intense frontal burst creates slightly blown-out specular highlights across his forehead, nose tip, exposed chest, the crisp white shirt collar, and the car's chrome details, before falling off rapidly into the deep background. Pitch black shadows with incredibly sharp, defined edges are cast abruptly under his shirt collar, beneath his arms, and directly behind his torso onto the mint green car door. The split-complementary color palette emphasizes the crisp whites and mint greens against the deep olive-black night. Captured on a 35mm lens at f/8, 1/160s, and ISO 400, the digital portrait channels a gritty 90s direct-flash editorial aesthetic. The image remains tack sharp across the subject and car side, while the deep background foliage subtly softens. Post-processing applies a high-contrast curve with crushed blacks, punchy highlights, a slightly cooled color temperature, and a touch of intentional film grain and digital noise lingering in the heavy shadows, all elegantly composed and framed in a 3:4 aspect ratio.
```

## Fallen Angel Fashion Editorial Grid

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra-photorealistic 2x2 luxury glossy editorial collage featuring the same woman across all four panels, with strict identity consistency, strict body consistency, the same skin tone, the same proportions, and the same elegant posture language in every frame.

CONCEPT:
A dark luxury fashion editorial centered on sculptural back lines, shoulders, neck, arms, and an open-back black dress, with a mysterious fallen-angel aura. The mood is sensual, elegant, expensive, cinematic, controlled, and minimal. The overall feeling should be high-fashion editorial, refined and dramatic, never cheap or costume-like.

SUBJECT:
A beautiful woman with long loose black hair, consistent identity across all four panels. Hair is sleek yet softly textured, flowing naturally down the back and shoulders. Makeup is dark smoky eye makeup with softly diffused black shadow, defined brows, and a polished editorial finish. Nails are glossy black, long and elegant. Expression and body language are poised, feminine, and powerful.

OUTFIT:
Deep black velvet or satin halter dress with an open back, realistic glossy fabric highlights, natural draping, subtle tension around the waist and hips, and elegant luxury construction. The dress must look premium and believable, with no fabric glitches.

WINGS:
Large {argument name="wing type" default="black angel wings"} behind the woman, dramatic and elegant, slightly torn and weathered for a fallen-angel feeling. The wings should feel sculptural and luxurious, not fantasy cosplay. Feather texture should be rich, layered, dark, and realistic, with softly damaged edges. The wings must integrate beautifully with the poses and lighting without overpowering the fashion editorial tone.

SKIN:
Glossy editorial wet-skin finish with a subtle oil sheen on the shoulders, upper back, neck, arms, and along the spine line. Real skin texture must remain visible, including pores, natural tonal variation, and realistic highlights. No plastic smoothing. The sheen should look expensive, controlled, and believable, not sweaty or messy.

ENVIRONMENT:
Minimal studio background with a {argument name="backdrop color" default="warm grey or beige"} seamless backdrop. No props, no distractions, no clutter.

LIGHTING:
Premium glossy studio lighting:
- large softbox key light from the upper side
- narrow rim light tracing the shoulders, spine, and wing edges
- soft warm highlights on skin and fabric
- deep but clean shadows
- smooth gradient falloff
- high-end editorial contrast
- no harsh flash
- no fake glow

GRID 2x2:

TOP LEFT:
Back-facing pose, arms raised above the head, hands resting in the hair. Spine line, shoulder blades, and upper wing structure are emphasized with glossy highlights. Black hair falls naturally over part of the back while the open-back black dress and wings remain clearly visible.

TOP RIGHT:
Slight torso twist, one shoulder closer to the camera, head turned subtly away. The black dress catches light along one side, and the black angel wings frame the silhouette elegantly. Stro
```

## Luxury Beach Club Vacation Portrait

```
A {argument name="subject" default="handsome young man"} sits squarely in the center of the frame on the edge of a daybed, leaning comfortably forward with his relaxed shoulders naturally hunched. He features dark hair, a neat goatee, and a broad, happy, open-mouthed smile revealing his upper teeth, his gaze directed off-camera to his right from behind round black sunglasses. His casual, {argument name="attire" default="upscale vacation attire consists of a crisp, bright white t-shirt, loose-fitting light cream trousers, and tan slide sandals"}. His hands are loosely clasped and resting naturally between his inner thighs, with the fingers of his left hand, which is adorned with a silver watch featuring a black dial, draped loosely over his right hand. He is seated on a wooden daybed featuring a slatted wood base and a soft gray textile cover, grounded on a light beige sand floor textured organically with subtle footprints. Receding into a deep, layered background of a {argument name="location" default="luxury beach club"}, clearly defined wooden cabanas frame the scene on the left and right, constructed with matte warm medium brown square pillars, smooth wood grain, and slatted roofs. Pristine, light beige sheer fabric curtains are elegantly tied back to the wooden posts alongside a closed canvas beach umbrella tied around a wooden pole on the center-right. A bright sky blue stretches overhead as highly directional, hard natural sunlight bathes the scene from the top front-left, creating a cheerful, sun-drenched summer atmosphere. This golden light preserves intense highlights on the top of his hair, the shoulders of his white t-shirt, his left arm, and the top of his thighs, while casting deep black to dark gray, sharply edged shadows beneath his chin, under his body, and projecting short-to-medium lengths onto the sand toward the bottom right. The high-contrast interplay between complementary warm sand-beige neutrals and cool blue accents brings a vibrant, structured aesthetic to the space with minimal ambient fill. Executed as a tack-sharp, grain-free digital photograph shot straight on at eye level with deep depth of field that keeps the receding cabanas relatively defined with only slight optical softening, the image channels modern luxury travel lifestyle photography, finalized with a crisp color grade that balances brilliant whites against warm wood tones, all beautifully framed in a 4:5 aspect ratio.
```

{argument name="subject" default="A young adult male with an athletic build and naturally textured two-inch dark curly hair"} stands with his weight casually shifted, facing the camera straight on in a sophisticated luxury editorial portrait. His serious, contemplative face features relaxed eyebrows and eyes gazing off to the left, drawing the viewer's eye down to the crisp {argument name="top" default="white collar of his dark navy blue polo shirt"}. He wears warm khaki trousers secured by a brown leather belt; his right hand rests deliberately on his waist with the thumb casually hooked over the leather and fingers draped softly against the fabric, while his relaxed left hand, adorned with a silver watch and accented by a gold ring and bracelet, drops casually to his side, partially cropped by the bottom edge of the frame. He is positioned right-center in an asymmetric composition, grounding the foreground before a {argument name="setting" default="deep, pristine outdoor luxury resort setting"}. Just behind him in the midground, a serene infinity pool features a solid gray-beige concrete underwater step and a perfectly calm surface that ripples gently, mirroring the twilight sky. Stretching into the deep background, the expansive space reveals a single, tall palm tree silhouetted on the left beside low-profile modern resort architecture accented by lush tropical foliage and warm glowing lights, while a rugged rocky coastal outcrop on the right slopes gracefully toward the distant ocean horizon. A calm, old-money atmospheric mood is established by the twilight evening, illuminated by a single, diffused setting sun from the front-left that casts a golden, warm light. This soft illumination perfectly preserves the highlights on the left side of his face, nose bridge, and left forearm, while creating short, soft-edged, gradual gray shadows across the right side of his face, neck, and right arm, gently lifted by an ambient white bounce fill. The split-complementary color palette richly contrasts the dark navy and khaki of his clothing against the soft teal-green water and the muted peach and gray gradient of the open sky. Captured as a tack-sharp realistic digital photograph on a full-frame mirrorless camera with a 50mm lens and an f/2.8 aperture at 1/200s and ISO 400, the medium depth of field keeps the subject distinctly crisp while smoothly blurring the deep background. Post-processing introduces a hint of cinematic digital noise and a subtle teal-and-peach color grading that lifts shadows for a softer film-like contrast while protecting the sky's gradient, all perfectly framed in a 4:5 aspect ratio.
```

Her face should appear like a real DSLR photograph with lifelike detail and cinematic realism. She is sitting confidently on a breathtaking floating miniature island inspired by the Philippines, suspended high in the sky. The island contains ultra-detailed tropical scenery including white sand beaches, crystal-clear turquoise lagoons, coconut palm trees, Banaue rice terraces, Chocolate Hills, Mayon Volcano, and dramatic El Nido limestone cliffs — all miniaturized yet photorealistic. Small waterfalls cascade from the edges of the island into soft clouds below, surrounded by atmospheric mist and depth. She is wearing a luxurious beige travel suit with a modern fitted blazer and tailored pants, paired with a black inner top and stylish brown leather boots. Realistic fabric folds and premium textures are visible. A pair of sleek wireless headphones rests naturally around her neck. Her pose is relaxed, confident, and natural, looking slightly toward the horizon with accurate anatomy and cinematic posture. Beside her are a matte black suitcase and a luxury tan leather backpack with highly detailed textures, enhancing the premium travel aesthetic. The floating island is surrounded by a bright blue sky filled with soft volumetric clouds. Two commercial airplanes fly far in the background with subtle contrails. Lighting is warm natural sunlight with cinematic softness, realistic shadows, HDR global illumination, and depth of field. Style: photorealistic, Unreal Engine 5 + Octane Render aesthetic, ultra-detailed, 8K, HDR lighting, physically accurate rendering, natural cinematic color grading, premium travel advertisement vibe. Negative prompts: cartoon, anime, CGI-looking skin, plastic face, over-smoothed skin, distorted anatomy, unrealistic proportions, low detail, blurry face, fake lighting

A male model stands centered and straight-on, exuding a serious, confident presence with his face featuring an even five o'clock shadow and a closed, neutral mouth, his gaze relaxed yet focused and directed away to the viewer's right. His hair is a three-to-four-inch medium-short classic taper cut with a layered top, swept back and parted slightly off-center to the left, styled with a matte paste that controls the naturally slightly wavy texture while revealing individual piecey strands, natural density variations, and minor organic flyaways along the hairline and crown for authentic imperfection. He is dressed in an {argument name="jacket style" default="oversized, textured dark espresso brown leather zip-up jacket"} with wide open collars over a crisp white crew-neck t-shirt, paired with warm tan trousers. His level shoulders and centered weight frame his meticulously positioned hands, both clad in dark espresso brown leather gloves; his left hand lightly pinches the left lens rim of a pair of round tortoiseshell sunglasses featuring dark purple-grey tinted lenses, while his right hand gently holds the right arm and hinge area with slightly curled fingers, suspending a fine gold-brass chain lanyard draped elegantly down across his torso. Directly behind him in the shallow midground stands a pristine outdoor architectural column made of smooth, matte painted plaster, boasting a crisp pure white surface and a perfect vertical corner line entirely devoid of scuffs or cracks. The scene is bathed in bright, highly directional natural sunlight striking from a forty-five-degree angle slightly top-down from the viewer's left, producing a dramatic, high-contrast atmosphere without any ambient fill. This hard light carves harsh, defined, deep black shadows under his jaw, beneath the jacket collar flaps, on the right side of his nose, and casts a heavy geometric shadow across the pristine white wall on the right, while meticulously preserving bright highlights on his forehead, nose bridge, right cheekbone, hair texture, and the top edges of the leather jacket shoulders. The overall mood is sophisticated and structured, heavily influenced by minimalist architectural fashion campaigns, balancing a neutral base with warm monochromatic accents. Captured as a high-end menswear editorial photograph, the image utilizes an 85mm lens at an aperture between f/5.6 and f/8, ISO 100, and a fast shutter speed to achieve tack-sharp, grain-free focus with a medium depth of field. The straight-on perspective is refined through post-processing with a high contrast curve, crisp sharpening to emphasize the leather grain and hair strands, and a neutral white balance ensuring deep, clean blacks, all perfectly framed in a 3:4 aspect ratio.
```

Barefoot. Accessories: {argument name="accessories" default="sunglasses MUST be present, simple elegant wristwatch on LEFT wrist, subtle delicate bracelet on RIGHT wrist"}. Hairstyle MUST be feminine and flowing, long wavy or straight hair gently moving in the breeze (match elegant feminine style suitable for the scene). Environment: calm beach, soft waves, lightly clouded sky, warm natural tones. Camera angle eye-level. No cropping tighter than full body.

## Mediterranean Riviera Editorial Portrait

> Sinematik, retro portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A confident young adult {argument name="subject" default="male with wavy dark hair"} is deliberately posed in a structured, layered composition, wearing a {argument name="outfit" default="light beige linen suit paired with a light blue unbuttoned dress shirt, a brown leather belt, brown loafers, and dark sunglasses"}. His relaxed expression features a neutral mouth and uncreased eyebrows as he looks thoughtfully away, his body turned at a 45-degree angle with a tilted shoulder line, leaning his weight to the right. His relaxed left hand rests naturally on the thicker top bar of a worn white metal railing, fingers lightly draped over the edge, while his right hand is casually tucked into his front right trouser pocket with only the thumb and wrist visible. He stands on an {argument name="location" default="outdoor Mediterranean cliffside terrace"} grounded by a checkered dark gray slate tile floor accented with blue and yellow decorative inserts, bordered by slightly textured, matte white plaster structural columns. In the foreground, an ornate vintage dark metal hanging lantern frames the top left, while the thin vertical bars of the white railing cross the midground. Beside him sits a dominant, weathered terracotta ornamental pot detailed with carved bands and handles, surrounded by subtle dry plant branches and green foliage, overlooking a lower weathered white corrugated roof. The deep spatial depth resolves into a softly blurred background showcasing a pristine deep ocean blue sea dotted with small white boats. The sun-drenched, sophisticated Old Hollywood Riviera atmosphere is shaped by bright, highly directional hard golden sunlight striking from the top left at a 45-degree angle. This single illumination source casts deep black, elongated, harsh-edged shadows onto the tiled floor and plaster walls from the subject, the railing, and the pot, while preserving crisp specular highlights on the subject's left shoulder, the edge of his face, and the white metal railing. Captured straight-on using realistic digital photography with a 50mm lens at f/5.6, ISO 100, and a 1/1000s shutter speed, the editorial image boasts a tack-sharp texture, medium depth of field, and cinematic film grain, enhanced by post-processing that applies warm highlights, cool crushed shadows, and a striking teal and orange complementary color contrast, elegantly framed in a 4:5 aspect ratio.
```

[scene_material_translation_engine]
vector_g (scene geometry) = "{argument name="scene" default="[insert any scene here]"}"
matrix_m (material world) = "paper craft materials: origami paper, folded cardstock, vellum, paper fibers, layered paper cutouts"
equation: vector_g ⊗ matrix_m = render_target
[scene_decomposition_rules]
the ai must infer all structural elements of {argument name="scene" default="[insert any scene here]"} and translate them into a pure paper-engineering system.
[mapping_constraints]
- replace 100% of the scene materials with paper-based materials only.
- every form must feel folded, creased, cut, layered, rolled, slotted, or assembled from paper.
[composition]
macro paper-sculpture photography. the entire scene appears as a miniature paper diorama on a matte black tabletop.
[visual_execution]
render the render_target. lighting: clean directional studio light. texture: visible paper grain, sharp creases, soft vellum translucency, layered cut-paper edges.
```

## Dual-frame fashion editorial layout

> Fotogerçekçi moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create an ultra realistic high resolution dual-frame fashion editorial image featuring the same woman presented in two complementary angles within a single composition, emphasizing pose variation, garment tension, and fabric behavior. The subject’s face, facial structure, skin tone, and body proportions must be strictly taken from the FIRST REFERENCE IMAGE with complete identity consistency and zero drift. COMPOSITION: The image is divided into two vertical sections:LEFT FRAME (Primary Full Body Shot): - full body view from head to knees - camera positioned at mid-torso height - slight three-quarter front angle - subject standing with weight shifted to one leg, creating a natural hip pop - legs close together with a subtle bend in one knee - torso upright but relaxed - one hand holding a phone in a mirror-style pose - other hand holding a small handbag RIGHT FRAME (Secondary Close-Up): - waist-up crop - side-back angle (approximately 60–75 degrees) shoulder rotated away from camera - back exposed - head turned back toward camera with direct eye contact OUTFIT DETAILS: The subject is wearing a {argument name="dress color" default="red"} {argument name="fabric" default="velvet"} halter mini dress. Top section: - deep plunging neckline - halter straps pulling upward creating visible vertical tension - bust area slightly compressed and lifted - subtle stretch lines radiating from neckline Waist: - tightly cinched - strong inward compression - slight fabric gathering along side seams Skirt: - short flared mini skirt- lightweight structured fabric - natural outward flare from waistline - radial folds forming from waistband - hemline slightly lifted due to posture - soft movement, not stiff Back (visible in right frame): - fully backless design - fabric pulled taut across lower back - subtle horizontal tension lines FOOTWEAR: - knee-high boots - matte finish - snug fit around calves - slight creasing at ankle due to stance HAIR: - long sleek high ponytail - falling straight down the back- smooth texture with clean edges LIGHTING: - soft indoor lighting - evenly distributed illumination - minimal harsh shadows - natural skin tone rendering CAMERA SETTINGS: - 50mm lens look - sharp focus on subject - clean neutral background The final image must feel like a polished fashion lookbook layout, with strong emphasis on dual-angle presentation, realistic fabric tension, and accurate garment structure.
```
